                    const root = document.getElementById('ai-chat');
                    const panel = document.getElementById('chatkit-panel');
                    const fab = document.getElementById('chatkit-fab');
                    const chatkit = document.getElementById('chatkit-assistant');
                    const closeX = document.getElementById('chatkit-close-x');
                    const closeHandle = document.getElementById('chatkit-close-handle');
                    const errorBox = document.getElementById('chatkit-assistant-error');
                    const sessionEndpoint = '/api/chatkit/session';
                    let isChatOpen = false;
                    let lastToggleAt = 0;
                    let hideTimer = null;
                    const CHATKIT_ANIMATION_MS = 280;

                    function openChat() {
                        if (!chatkit) {
                            return;
                        }

                        if (hideTimer) {
                            clearTimeout(hideTimer);
                            hideTimer = null;
                        }

                        chatkit.removeAttribute('hidden');

                        // Kick transition on next frame after hidden/display state updates.
                        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                            chatkit.classList.add('is-visible');
                        });
                    }

                    function closeChat() {
                        if (!chatkit) {
                            return;
                        }

                        chatkit.classList.remove('is-visible');

                        if (hideTimer) {
                            clearTimeout(hideTimer);
                        }

                        hideTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                            if (!isChatOpen) {
                                chatkit.setAttribute('hidden', 'hidden');
                            }
                        }, CHATKIT_ANIMATION_MS);
                    }

                    function renderChatState() {
                        if (!chatkit || !fab || !panel || !root) {
                            return;
                        }

                        if (isChatOpen) {
                            openChat();
                            fab.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
                            fab.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Close chat panel');
                            root.classList.add('is-open');
                            root.classList.remove('is-collapsed');
                            panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
                        } else {
                            closeChat();
                            fab.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                            fab.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Open chat panel');
                            root.classList.remove('is-open');
                            root.classList.add('is-collapsed');
                            panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
                        }
                    }

                    function setChatState(nextState, event) {
                        if (event) {
                            event.preventDefault();
                        }

                        // Guard against double-firing across touch/click/pointer on mobile browsers.
                        const now = Date.now();
                        if (now - lastToggleAt < 250) {
                            return;
                        }
                        lastToggleAt = now;

                        isChatOpen = nextState;
                        renderChatState();
                    }

                    async function getClientSecret(_currentClientSecret) {
                        const response = await fetch(sessionEndpoint, {
                            method: 'POST',
                            headers: {
                                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                            },
                        });

                        if (!response.ok) {
                            let message = `ChatKit session request failed (${response.status}).`;
                            try {
                                const payload = await response.json();
                                if (payload?.error) {
                                    message = payload.error;
                                }
                            } catch (error) {
                                // Use default message if the error response isn't JSON.
                            }
                            throw new Error(message);
                        }

                        const payload = await response.json();
                        if (!payload?.client_secret) {
                            throw new Error('ChatKit session missing client_secret.');
                        }

                        return payload.client_secret;
                    }

                    function applyChatkitInternalMarginOverride() {
                        let applied = false;

                        // Try open shadow root first.
                        const shadowRoot = chatkit?.shadowRoot;
                        if (shadowRoot && !shadowRoot.getElementById('chatkit-margin-override')) {
                            const style = document.createElement('style');
                            style.id = 'chatkit-margin-override';
                            style.textContent = '.d091K { margin-left: 40px !important; } .Q8u4H { display: none !important; }';
                            shadowRoot.appendChild(style);
                            applied = true;
                        } else if (shadowRoot?.getElementById('chatkit-margin-override')) {
                            applied = true;
                        }

                        // If ChatKit is rendered in an iframe and same-origin is allowed, inject there too.
                        try {
                            const iframe = chatkit?.querySelector?.('iframe');
                            const iframeDoc = iframe?.contentDocument;
                            if (iframeDoc?.head && !iframeDoc.getElementById('chatkit-margin-override')) {
                                const style = iframeDoc.createElement('style');
                                style.id = 'chatkit-margin-override';
                                style.textContent = '.d091K { margin-left: 40px !important; } .Q8u4H { display: none !important; }';
                                iframeDoc.head.appendChild(style);
                                applied = true;
                            } else if (iframeDoc?.getElementById('chatkit-margin-override')) {
                                applied = true;
                            }
                        } catch (_error) {
                            // Cross-origin iframe: cannot inject styles there from host page.
                        }

                        return applied;
                    }

                    function bindToggleControl(element) {
                        if (!element) {
                            return;
                        }

                        // Pointer/touch handles direct taps. Keep click only for keyboard activation.
                        if (window.PointerEvent) {
                            element.addEventListener('pointerup', (event) => {
                                if (element === fab) {
                                    setChatState(true, event);
                                } else {
                                    setChatState(false, event);
                                }
                            });
                            element.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
                                if (event.detail === 0) {
                                    if (element === fab) {
                                        setChatState(true, event);
                                    } else {
                                        setChatState(false, event);
                                    }
                                }
                            });
                        } else {
                            element.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
                                if (element === fab) {
                                    setChatState(true, event);
                                } else {
                                    setChatState(false, event);
                                }
                            });
                            element.addEventListener('touchend', (event) => {
                                if (element === fab) {
                                    setChatState(true, event);
                                } else {
                                    setChatState(false, event);
                                }
                            }, { passive: false });
                        }
                    }

                    async function initChatKit() {
                        if (!chatkit || !fab || !window.customElements) {
                            return;
                        }

                        bindToggleControl(fab);
                        bindToggleControl(closeX);

                        renderChatState();

                        try {
                            await window.customElements.whenDefined('openai-chatkit');

                            chatkit.setOptions({
                                api: {
                                    async getClientSecret(currentClientSecret) {
                                        // For managed ChatKit, we mint short-lived session secrets on our backend endpoint.
                                        // TODO: If you want explicit refresh semantics, add a refresh endpoint and use currentClientSecret.
                                        return getClientSecret(currentClientSecret);
                                    },
                                },
                            });

                            // ChatKit UI can mount asynchronously; retry a few times to apply internal margin override.
                            let attempts = 0;
                            const maxAttempts = 24;
                            const interval = setInterval(() => {
                                attempts += 1;
                                const done = applyChatkitInternalMarginOverride();
                                if (done || attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                                    clearInterval(interval);
                                }
                            }, 250);
                        } catch (error) {
                            console.error('Unable to initialize ChatKit:', error);
                            if (chatkit) {
                                chatkit.setAttribute('hidden', 'hidden');
                            }
                            if (fab) {
                                fab.setAttribute('hidden', 'hidden');
                            }
                            if (errorBox) {
                                errorBox.hidden = false;
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    initChatKit();
